財務回顧                                           Financial Review
業績摘要                                           Results Highlights
 截 至 2021 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度 ， 本 集 團 錄 得 約      For the year ended 31 December 2021, the Group recorded revenue of

匯率波動風險及相關對沖活動                        Exposure to Fluctuations in Exchange Rates and Related
                                     Hedges
本集團的功能貨幣為人民幣 ，且大多數交易以人               The Group’s functional currency is RMB with most of the transactions
民幣結算 。然而 ，本集團自海外客戶賺取收益及              settled in RMB. However, foreign currencies (mainly United States dollars)
向海外供應商購買機器及設備結算時均收取╱支                were received/paid when the Group earned revenue from overseas
付外幣（主要為美元）。                          customers and when settling purchases of machinery and equipment
                                     from the overseas suppliers.


為減少持有外幣的風險 ，本集團經考慮不久將來               In order to reduce the risk of holding foreign currencies, the Group normally
的外幣付款安排後 ，通常會在款項收訖後隨即將               converts the foreign currencies into RMB upon receipt while taking into
外幣兌換成人民幣 。                           account its foreign currencies payment schedule in the near future.

東岳集團有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事會」）    The board of Directors (the “Board”) of Dongyue Group Limited (the
肩負為本公司股東（「股東」）創造價值及帶來最大      “Company”) recognizes their mission of creating values and maximizing
回報的使命 ，同時履行其企業責任 。為此 ，本公     returns to the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholders”), while
司矢志提升及保持最高水準的企業管治 。          at the same time fulfilling their corporate responsibilities. To this end,
                             we strive to promote and uphold the highest standard of corporate
                             governance.


董事會已檢討本公司的企業管治常規 ，並採納及       The Board has reviewed the corporate governance practices of
改進各項程序及文書處理方式 ，詳情載於本報        the Company with the adoption and improvement of the various
告 。據董事所盡知及盡信 ，本公司於截至2021年    procedures and documentation which are detailed in this report. To
12月31日止年度及截至本報告日期採納並一直遵      the best knowledge and belief of the Directors, the Company has
守上市規則附錄14所載的企業管治守則（「守則」）     adopted and complied with the code provisions of the Corporate
的守則條文 ，惟關於主席與行政總裁的角色偏離       Governance Code contained in Appendix 14 of the Listing Rules (the
守則除外 ，有關詳情披露於本報告下文相關段        “Code”) during the year ended 31 December 2021 and up to the date
落。                           of this report, except for deviation regarding the roles of chairman and
                             chief executive officer, the details of which have been disclosed in the
                             relevant paragraphs below in this report.

和表現 。董事會轉授權力和責任予本集團管理人       businesses, strategic direction and performance. The Board delegates

技能及經驗方面實現良好平衡 ，符合本集團業務       the Board is reviewed regularly to ensure that it has a good balance of

主席與行政總裁的角色                  Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守則第二部分第C.2.1條規定 ，主席與行政總裁的   Code C.2.1 of Part 2 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman
角色應有區分 ，且不應由同一人兼任 。張建宏      and chief executive officer should be separate and should not be
先生目前身兼本公司主席與行政總裁 。董事會認      performed by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the
為 ，由一人同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團      Chairman and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board
更能貫徹強勢領導 ，且在業務策劃上更具效率 。     believes that vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive
此外 ，董事會認為 ，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹     Officer in the same position provides the Group with stronger and more
的人士所組成董事會密切監控下 ，此架構不會影      consistent leadership and allows for more effective planning. Further,
響權力平衡 。董事會對張先生充滿信心 ，並深信     the Board considers that this structure will not impair the balance
現時安排對本集團的業務前景最為有利 。         of power, which has been closely monitored by the Board, which
                            comprises experienced and high caliber individuals. The Board has full
                            confidence in Mr. Zhang and believes that the current arrangement is
                            beneficial to the business prospect of the Group.


董事的委任及重選                    Appointment and re-election of Directors
新董事的委任及其他相關事項已委派予本公司        The appointment of new Director(s) and other related matters have
提名委員會負責 。提名委員會就相關事項（包括      been delegated to the Company’s Nomination Committee, which
本公司董事的委任及重新委任）向董事會提供建       would make recommendations to the Board on relevant matters
議 。董事會認為 ，預期新任董事應具備有關範疇     including the appointment or reappointment of the Directors of the
的專業知識 ，以為本公司作出貢獻 ，且有足夠時     Company. The Board considers that the new Director(s) is expected to
間參與本公司的決策程序 。               have expertise in relevant area to make contribution to the Company
                            and to have sufficient time to participate in the decision making
                            process of the Company.

董事會聲明                                  Board Statement
本聲明旨在闡釋東岳集團董事會對環境 、社會及                 The purpose of this statement is to explain the governance and supervision
管治相關工作的治理和監督責任 。                       responsibilities of the Board of the Dongyue Group with respect to ESG
                                       related work.


東岳集團有限公司始終認為公司管治和風險管理                  Dongyue Group Limited has always believed that corporate governance
是企業可持續發展的必要環節 。集團在可持續發                 and risk management are essential components of sustainable corporate
展的過程中 ，不斷提升對環境 、社會及管治三個                development. In the process of sustainable development, the Group
維度的關注度和投入度 ，將企業發展與社會 、環                continues to enhance its concern and commitment to the three
境高度融合 ，助力良好的行業發展 ，以自身資源                dimensions of ESG, integrating corporate development with society and
帶動環境改善 ，與社會共同發展 ，共創價值 。                the environment, contributing to good industry development, driving
                                       environmental improvement with its own resources, developing together
                                       with society and creating value together.

本集團開展以董事會為負責人的ESG工作管理架       The Group has put in place a management structure for its ESG work with
構 。董事會重點關注東岳集團有限公司可持續發       the Board as the responsible party. The Board focuses on the sustainable
展表現 ，制定公司整體ESG戰略 ，聽取ESG管理工   development performance of Dongyue Group Limited, formulates the
作匯報 ，並對管理成果進行審核 ，跟進ESG工作     overall ESG strategy of the Company, receives reports on ESG management
目標的制定及完成情況 。ESG工作小組作為本集      work, reviews management results and follows up on the formulation and
團投資者關係部下設機構 ，日常負責ESG工作的      achievement of ESG work targets. The ESG working group, as a division
推進與落實 ，對ESG各範疇相關風險進行管控 。     of the Group’s Investor Relations Department, is responsible for the day-
公司基於內外部經濟社會宏觀環境和公司發展戰        to-day implementation and execution of ESG work and the management
略 ，定期開展ESG重要議題評估 ，圍繞公司在ESG   of risks related to various aspects of ESG. Based on the domestic and
方面的風險和機遇進行討論 ，將重點議題的管理       international economic and social macro environment and the Company’s
與提升作為ESG年度重點工作 。同時舉辦內外部      development strategy, the Company conducts regular assessments of
活動 ，加強與利益相關方溝通 。本集團遵照香港      important ESG issues, discusses the risks and opportunities of the Company
聯交所關於ESG指引文件發佈ESG年度報告 ，董事    in relation to ESG, and considers the management and enhancement
會負責ESG報告審閱與確認工作 。            of key issues as an annual ESG priority. At the same time, the Company
                             organized internal and external events to enhance communication with
                             stakeholders. The Group publishes annual ESG reports in compliance with
                             the Hong Kong Stock Exchange’s guideline documents on ESG, and the
                             Board is responsible for the review and validation of ESG reports.


2021年 ，董事會對以下重要議題的核心工作進行     In 2021, the Board has conducted major review and supervision on the core
重點審視與監督：研發創新 、生產安全 、合規運      tasks of the following key issues, including R&D innovation, production
營 、綠色產品 、節能減排等 。             safety, compliance operation green products, and energy saving and
                             emission reduction.

ESG管理                                       ESG Management
東岳集團為保證可持續發展行之有效 ，建立了由                      In order to ensure effective sustainable development, Dongyue Group has
董事會領導 、工作小組組織 、相關職能部門執行                     established an ESG management system led by the Board and organized by
的ESG管理體系 ，統籌各職責部門的工作機制 ，                    a working group and executed by relevant functional departments, which
明確各層級的工作職責 ，使集團的可持續發展工                      coordinates the working mechanism of each responsible department and
作更加專業化 、系統化 、規範化 。                          clarifies the working responsibilities at each level, allowing the Group’s
                                            sustainable development work to be more professional, systematic and
                                            standardized.


                           公司董事會參與社會責任重大事項的審議與決策，包括相關戰略與方針的制定、對

      Decision-making      The Board of the Company reviewed and decided on major social responsibility matters,
                           including formulating relevant strategies and guidelines and performing special reviews
                           on the annual ESG report.

                           公司在投資者關係部設立專門工作小組，負責協調日常ESG管理工作，包括編製年度
                           環境、社會及管治報告、ESG績效指針收集、組織開展培訓倡導等。
           組織層             The Company established a special task group in the investor relations department for
        Organization       coordinating ESG management related tasks on a daily basis, including the preparation
                           of the annual ESG report, collection of ESG performance indicators and organization of
                           training and promotion.

                           公司各職能部門及分子公司負責各項ESG議題的歸口管理，定期報送相關管理舉措、

                           activities.

     4.1 氣候風險管理                           4.1 Climate Change Risk Analysis
       氣候變化為經濟和社會發展帶來的影響                       The impact of climate change on economic and social
       深遠 ，是當前全球共同面對的環境挑                       development is profound and is a common global environmental
       戰 。面對刻不容緩的氣候變化形勢 ，                      challenge. In the face of the urgent climate change situation,
       東岳集團關注氣候變化為公司業務帶來                       Dongyue Group pays attention to the impact of climate change
       的影響 ，將對氣候風險的考量融入現                       on its business, integrates the consideration of climate risk into
       有風險管理體系 ，積極識別並評估氣                       its existing risk management system, and actively identifies and
       候變化面臨的風險和機遇 。                           evaluates the risks and opportunities faced by climate change.

•   面臨的氣候變化風險                                            •    Exposure to climate change risk
    轉型風險             政策風險           2021年，中國將「碳達峰」「碳中和」首次寫入政府工作報告。碳達峰、碳
                                    中和目標的提出，不僅是世界各國對碳排放量的約束，也意味著經濟社會發
                                    展方式的變革。首先是追求產業結構的低碳化，其次是培育清潔化和低碳化
                                    的新型產業趨勢，在今後相當長時期內不會發生改變。

                                    東岳集團作為首批納入碳交易試點的為發電企業，考慮到未來控排範圍的持
                                    續擴大，公司持續推進減排技術創新，推出新型環境友好產品，降低因限排
                                    政策變化帶來的風險。
    Transition risks Policy risks   In 2021, China included “carbon peaking” and “carbon neutral” in the government
                                    work report for the first time. The proposal of carbon peak and carbon neutral
                                    targets is not only a constraint on carbon emissions around the world, but also
                                    implies a change in the way of economic and social development. The first is
                                    to pursue the low carbonization of the industrial structure, and the second is to
                                    cultivate a new industrial trend of cleanliness and low carbonization, which will
                                    not change for a long time in the future.


                                    As one of the first power generation enterprises to be included in the carbon
                                    trading pilot project, Dongyue Group continues to promote emission reduction
                                    technology innovation and introduce new environmentally friendly products to
                                    reduce the risk brought about by changes in emission restriction policies, taking
                                    into account the continuous expansion of the scope of emission control in the
                                    future.


                     市場風險           客戶對綠色產品方案和低碳足跡產品的需求不斷增加，海外標準對製冷劑要
                                    求的生產標準較高。東岳集團在產品生產時若未能嚴格控制排放物排量，選
                                    擇低碳供應鏈，研發新型環境友好產品，可能會因消費者選擇影響市場銷售
                                    額。
                     Market risks   Market risk customers’ demand for green product solutions and low carbon
                                    footprint products is constantly increasing, and overseas standards require higher
                                    production standards for refrigerants. If Dongyue Group fails to strictly control
                                    emissions during product production, choose a low carbon supply chain, and
                                    develop new environmentally friendly products, it may affect market sales due to
                                    consumer choice.

           實體風險             物理風險             全球氣候變化導致出現的暴雨、颱風、沙塵暴、凝凍等極端天氣或強度更
                                             強、更加頻繁，並導致水資源短缺、乾旱等長期氣候變化，這將潛在地影響
                                             上游供應鏈穩定生產生產及原材料的運輸儲存，對公司生產設備的安全造成
                                             威脅，導致產能下降增加公司運營成本。
           Physical risks   Physical risks   Global climate change may lead to more intense and frequent extreme weather
                                             such as rainstorms, typhoons, dust storms, and freezing temperatures, as well
                                             as long-term climate changes such as water shortages and droughts. This will
                                             potentially affect the stable production of upstream supply chain and the
                                             transportation and storage of raw materials, threaten the safety of the Company’s
                                             production facilities and lead to a decrease in production capacity and increase
                                             the Company’s operating costs.

      4.3 提高能源效率                          4.3 Enhancing Energy Efficiency

4.5 減少污染排放             4.5 Reduction of Pollutant Emissions
 東岳集團嚴格遵守運營所在地排放標         Dongyue Group strictly abided by the emission standards
 準 ，力爭實現污染排放最小化 。公司       where it operated and strived to minimize pollutant emissions.
 嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國環境保護法》       The Company strictly abided by the Environmental Protection
 《中華人民共和國大氣污染防治法》《中       Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Law of the
 華人民共和國水污染防治法》《固體廢物       People’s Republic of China on the Prevention and Control
 污染環境防治法》 《噪聲污染環境防治       of Atmospheric Pollution, the Law of the People’s Republic
 法》等相關法律 ，建立相應的排放管理       of China on the Prevention and Control of Environmental
 規程 ，通過設備更新和技術創新 ，對       Pollution by Solid Waste, the Law of the People’s Republic
 廢水 、廢氣 、噪聲和固體廢物實施排       of China on the Prevention and Control of Noise Pollution
 放控制與檢測 ，確保「三廢」排放依法       and other relevant laws, established corresponding emission
 合規 ，減少排放對環境造成的負擔 。       management regulations through the equipment renewal and
                          technological innovation. It implemented emission control and
                          inspection for wastewater, exhaust gas, noise and solid waste to
                          ensure compliance with laws and regulations for the emissions
                          of the above three types of wastes, and reduced the burden
                          of emissions on the environment.


  東岳集團嚴格按照《排污許可證》管理要      Dongyue Group fully implements the management
  求全面落實 ，要求從公司到車間層層       requirements of the Emission Permit in strict accordance with the
  落實 ，執行到位 ，做好例行監測和運      requirements of the Emission Permit, requiring implementation
  行台賬日常監管 ，按照管理要求將執       at all levels from the Company to the workshop, execution
  行落實情況提交排污許可系統公開 。       in place, routine monitoring and daily supervision of the
  東岳集團將持續推進環保減排舉措 ，       operation ledger, and submission of the implementation and
  至2025年實現廢棄物排放全部合規處      execution to the Emission Permit system for public disclosure
  置 ，三廢排放達標率達到100% 。      in accordance with the management requirements. Dongyue
                          Group will continue to promote environmental protection and
                          emission reduction initiatives to achieve all compliant disposal
                          of waste emissions and 100% compliance rate of three waste
                          emissions by 2025.

5. 和諧共贏                   5. All-win Harmony
 東岳集團堅信人才是公司成功發展的基礎 ，        Dongyue Group always regards talents as the cornerstone for the
 奉行「以效益體現價值 ，用財富回報才智」的       success of its development, while adhering to the talent concept
 人才理念 ，實現公司與員工的雙贏 。本集團       of “reflecting values with benefits and using wealth to reward
 重視平等和非歧視的用工政策 ，在招聘 、薪       intelligence” in order to achieve a win-win situation for the company
 酬績效 、福利和勞工權益方面優化人力資源        and its employees. The Group strongly advocates an equal and non-
 配置 ，提升內部管理 ，持續對員工進行職業       discriminatory employment policy, optimizes its human resources
 技能和職業健康培訓 ，不斷培養行業領軍人        arrangement in recruitment, remuneration and performance,
 才 ，為員工創造多元 、平等 、向上的工作       benefits and labour rights, enhances its internal management. Also,
 環境 。                        it constantly provides employees with trainings on vocational skills
                             and occupational health and continuously nurtures industry leaders,
                             creating a diverse, equal and uplifting working environment for
                             employees.

   東岳集團堅持讓所有員工在招聘 、晉              Dongyue Group perseveres in its equal treatment towards all

   法規 ，堅持平等合規僱傭 ，公平對待             the People’s Republic of China and other national laws and
   不同性別 、地域 、年齡 、民族 、宗教           regulations, pursued fair and legal employment practices,

  健康 ，減輕工作壓力 。                         meals and other employee benefits, and also values employee

  年 ，本集團結合集團經營現狀和市場                    demand of the Group due to its rapid development. In 2021,

                                              INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT




 致東岳集團有限公司全體股東                          To the Shareholders of Dongyue Group Limited
（於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）                        (incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)


意見                                      Opinion
 吾等已審核第135至277頁所載東岳集團有限公司               We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Dongyue
（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「貴集團」）的綜                Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively referred
 合財務報表，當中包括於2021年12月31日的綜合              to as the “Group”) set out on pages 135 to 277, which comprise
 財務狀況表及截至該日止年度的綜合損益及其他                  the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31 December
 全面收入報表、綜合權益變動表及綜合現金流量                  2021, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
 表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策                  comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes in
 概要。                                    equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then
                                        ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a
                                        summary of significant accounting policies.


吾等認為，綜合財務報表已遵照國際會計準則理                   In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and
事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的國際財務報                 fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31
告準則（「國際財務報告準則」）真實公平地反映貴                 December 2021, and of its consolidated financial performance and
集團於2021年12月31日之綜合財務狀況，及其於               its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance
截至該日止年度之綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量                   with International Financial Reporting Standards (“IFRSs”) issued by
及該等綜合財務報表已按照香港《公司條例》的披                  the International Accounting Standards Board (“IASB”) and have been
露規定妥為編製。                                properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure requirements of
                                        the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


意見的基礎                                   Basis for Opinion
吾等已根據香港會計師公會（「香港會計師公會」）                 We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on
頒佈之香港審計準則（「香港審計準則」）進行審                  Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public
計。吾等在該等準則下承擔的責任已在本報告                    Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our responsibilities under those standards
「核數師就審核綜合財務報表承擔的責任」部分中                  are further described in the “Auditor’s Responsibilities for the Audit of

附註：                                         Notes:

(a)   股份溢價                                  (a)      Share premium

      根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），本公司的股份溢價賬可                      Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands, the share premium
      分配給本公司所有者，惟在緊接建議分派股息之日後，                       of the Company is distributable to the owners of the Company provided that
      本公司將有能力償還其在日常業務過程中到期的債務。                       immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to be distributed,
                                                     the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts as they fall due in the ordinary
                                                     course of business.

(b)   按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備                   (b)      Equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve

      按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備包括於報告                       The equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve comprises the cumulative net change in
      期末根據國際財務報告準則第9號持有之按公平值計入其                      the fair value of equity instruments at FVTOCI under IFRS 9 that are held at the end of
      他全面收益之股本投資之公平值累計淨變動。                           the reporting period.

(c)   合併儲備                                  (c)      Merger reserve

      合併儲備來自於2006年完成之集團重組。                           Merger reserve arose in group reorganisation completed in 2006.

(d)   股本儲備                                  (d)      Capital reserve

      於2007年11月16日，本公司購回所有早前已發行每股面                   On 16 November 2007, the Company repurchased all of the 275,000,000 previously
      值0.1美元之普通股275,000,000股，而該等購回普通股與               issued ordinary shares of US$0.1 each and these repurchased ordinary shares were
      截至該日期之所有法定但未發行股本一併註銷。同日，                       cancelled with all of the authorised but unissued share capital as of that date. On
      藉增設4,000,000,000股每股面值0.1港元之新普通股，將法             the same date, the authorised share capital was increased to HK$400,000,000 by
      定股本增至400,000,000港元。於2007年11月15日，向現有            the creation of 4,000,000,000 new ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each. 275,000,000 new
      股東發行275,000,000股每股面值0.1港元之新普通股。購               ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each were then issued to the shareholders existing on 15
      回金額超過已發行新股份面值之款額直接計入股本儲備。                      November 2007. The excess of the repurchased amount over the nominal amount of
                                                     new shares issued was credited directly to the capital reserve.

      向附屬公司非控股股東收購額外權益確認為與非控股股                       The acquisition of additional interest from non-controlling shareholders of subsidiaries
      東權益之交易，因此產生之相關折讓╱溢價直接於股本                       were recognised as transactions with non-controlling interests and the corresponding
      儲備列賬╱支銷。                                       discount/premium were credited/debited directly against capital reserve.

(e)   安全儲備                                  (e)      Safety reserve

      根據中國財政部及國家安全生產監督管理總局頒佈的若                       Pursuant to certain regulations issued by the Ministry of Finance and the State
      干法規，本集團之若干中國附屬公司須以累進比率按危                       Administration of Work Safety of the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”), some of the
      險化學品銷售總收入之0.5%至4%預留一定數額之安全儲                    Group’s PRC subsidiaries are required to set aside an amount of safety reserve at
      備金。該儲備可用作集團日常運作於安全生產改善及維                       progressive rates from 0.5% to 4% of the total revenue from the sales of hazardous
      修之用，被視為開支性質，並於產生時計入損益。                         chemical. The reserve can be utilised for the spending in improvements and
                                                     maintenances of work safety on the Group’s daily operations, which are considered
                                                     expenses in nature and charged to the profit and loss as incurred.

(f)   法定盈餘儲備                                (f)      Statutory surplus reserve

      根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）公司法及相關公司組織章                     In accordance with the Company Law of PRC and the relevant Articles of Association,
      程細則，本公司中國附屬公司須將相等於根據中國會計                       the PRC subsidiaries of the Company are required to appropriate amount equal
      準則釐定之除稅後溢利10%之金額撥入法定盈餘儲備。                      to 10% of their profit after taxation as determined in accordance with the PRC
                                                     accounting standards to the statutory surplus reserve.

      法定盈餘儲備為股東權益之一部分，當其結餘達到相當                       Statutory surplus reserve is part of shareholders’ equity and when its balance reaches
      於註冊資本50%之金額，則毋須進一步分配。根據中國公                     an amount equal to 50% of the registered capital, further appropriation is not
      司法，法定盈餘儲備可用作填補過往虧損、增加生產及                       required. According to the Company Law of the PRC, statutory surplus reserve may
      業務營運或轉增股本。                                     be used to make up past losses, to increase production and business operations or to
                                                     increase capital by means of conversion.

(g)   截至2020年12月31日止年度，本集團進一步向少數股東          (g)      During the year ended 31 December 2020, the Group further acquired 36% of the
      收購山東東岳聯邦置業有限公司（「聯邦置業」）36%股權。                   equity interests of Shandong Dongyue Union Property Co., Ltd. (“Union Property”),
      於完成收購後，聯邦置業成為本集團之全資附屬公司。                       from the minority shareholders. Upon completion of acquisition, Union Property
                                                     became a wholly-owned subsidiary of the Group.

       本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司及其附                             The Company is an investment holding company. The Company
       屬公司（合稱「本集團」）主要從事製造、分銷                           and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) are
       及銷售高分子材料、有機硅、製冷劑及二氯                             principally engaged in the manufacture, distribution and sale

3. 主要會計政策                     3. Significant accounting policies
 報告編製依據                            Basis of preparation
 綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事會頒               The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
 佈的國際財務報告準則編製。另外，綜合財               accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards issued
 務報表包括香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上               by International Accounting Standards Board. In addition, the
 市規則及香港公司條例規定之適用披露。                consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures
                                   required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The
                                   Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the Hong Kong
                                   Companies Ordinance.

 綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製，惟金               The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on
 融工具於各報告期末乃按公平值計入損益及               the historical cost basis except for financial instruments that are
 按公平值計入其他全面收益計量。歷史成本               measured at fair value through profit or loss and fair value through
 一般按交換貨品及服務之代價之公平值計                other comprehensive income at the end of each reporting
 算。                                period. Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the
                                   consideration given in exchange for goods and services.

3. 主要會計政策（續）                             3. Significant accounting policies (Continued)
       報告編製依據（續）                             Basis of preparation (Continued)
       公平值指於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交                   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
       易中出售一項資產可收取或轉讓一項負債須                   paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market
       支付之價格，不論該價格是否直接可觀察或                   participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that
       可使用其他估值方法作出估計。倘市場參與                   price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation
       者於計量日期對資產或負債定價時會考慮資                   technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability,
       產或負債的特點，則本集團於估計資產或負                   the Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or
       債的公平值時會考慮該等特點。該等綜合財                   liability if market participants would take those characteristics into
       務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途的公平值乃                   account when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement
       按此基準釐定，惟屬於國際財務報告準則第                   date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in
       2號以股份支付款項範圍的以股份付款的交                   these consolidated financial statements is determined on such a
       易、屬於國際財務報告準則第16號範圍內的                  basis, except for share-based payment transactions that are within
       租賃交易，以及與公平值有部分相若地方但                   the scope of IFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that
       並非公平值的計量，譬如國際會計準則第2                   are accounted for in accordance with IFRS 16, and measurements
       號存貨內的可變現淨值或國際會計準則第36                  that have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such
       號資產減值的使用價值除外。                         as net realisable value in IAS 2 Inventories or value in use in IAS 36
                                             Impairment of Assets.


       此外，出於財務報告目的，公平值計量應基                   In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
       於公平值計量的輸入值的可觀察程度以及該                   measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
       等輸入值對整體公平值計量的重要性，被歸                   degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
       入第一層、第二層或第三層的公平值級次，                   observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
       詳述如下：                                 measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:


       第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產或                   Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
       負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得出；                  for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
       第二層輸入值是指由除了第一層輸入值所包                   measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted
       含的報價以外的，資產或負債可由直接或間                   prices included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
       接觀察得出的輸入值；及第三層輸入值是指                   or liability, either directly or indirectly; and Level 3 inputs are
       資產或負債的不可觀察輸入值。                        unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.

3. 主要會計政策（續）             3. Significant accounting policies (Continued)
 綜合基準                       Basis of consolidation
 綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及其附        The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
 屬公司控制的實體的財務報表。當本公司具        statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
 備以下條件時，即具有控制權：             Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                            Company:


 －   有控制被投資方的權力；            –    has power over the investee;


 －   可或有權自其與被投資方的關係中獲得      –    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
     不同回報；及                      involvement with the investee; and


 －   有能力運用其權力影響其回報。         –    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.


 倘事實和情況顯示上述的三項控制因素之一        The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
 項或多項出現變動，本集團會重新評估其是        facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or
 否控制被投資方。                   more of the three elements of control listed above.

 客戶合約收益                     Revenue from contracts with customers
 本集團於完成履約責任時，即於特定履約責        The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance
 任相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予客戶       obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or services
 時確認收益。                     underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred to
                            the customer.


 履約責任指可明確區分的一項貨品或服務         A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a
 （或一組貨品或服務），或一系列大致相同的       bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct
 可明確區分的貨品或服務。               goods or services that are substantially the same.


 倘符合以下其中一項標準，則控制權隨時間        Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over
 轉移，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任        time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of
 的進展情況而隨時間確認：               the relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria
                            is met:


 －   客戶在本集團履約的同時即取得並消耗      –    the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the
     本集團履約所帶來之利益；                benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group
                                 performs;


 －   本集團履約導致產生及提升一項資產，      –    the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that
     而該項資產於產生或提升時由客戶控            the customer controls as the Group performs; or
     制；或


 －   本集團履約未產生對本集團具有替代用      –    the Group’s performance does not create an asset with
     途之資產，而本集團有執行權收取迄今           an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an
     已履約部分之款項。                   enforceable right to payment for performance completed to
                                 date.


 否則，收益於客戶獲得特定貨品或服務的控        Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the
 制權時確認。                     customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.


 合約負債為本集團向客戶轉讓本集團已從客        A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer
 戶收取代價（或應從客戶收取代價金額）的貨       goods or services to a customer for which the Group has received
 品或服務的責任。                   consideration (or an amount of consideration is due) from the
                            customer.

       租賃                                    Leases
       租賃的定義                                 Definition of a lease
       倘合約授予權利以代價為交換在某一時期內                   A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right
       控制使用已識別資產，則該合約屬於租賃或                   to control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in
       包含租賃。                                 exchange for consideration.


       就於國際財務報告準則第16號初步應用日期                  For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of
       或之後訂立或修訂或由業務合併產生的合約                   initial application of IFRS 16 or arising from business combinations,
       而言，本集團根據國際財務報告準則第16號                  the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease
       中的定義，於開始、修訂日期或收購日期評                   based on the definition under IFRS 16 at inception, modification
       估合約是否為一項租賃或包含一項租賃。除                   date or acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be
       非合約條款及條件其後出現變動，否則有關                   reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract are
       合約將不予重新評估。                            subsequently changed.


       本集團作為承租人                              The Group as a lessee
       就可行實際權宜方法而言，當本集團合理預                   As a practical expedient, leases with similar characteristics are
       期對綜合財務報表的影響將不會與投資組合                   accounted on a portfolio basis when the Group reasonably
       內個別租賃有重大差異時，具有相似特徵的                   expects that the effects on the consolidated financial statements
       租賃將以組合為基礎入賬。                          would not differ materially from individual leases within the
                                             portfolio.

 資產，而該等資產需要一段長時間方可供用      construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets
 作擬定用途或出售。借貸成本會加入作該等      that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready for
 資產的成本，直至資產大致上可供用作擬定      their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those assets
 用途或出售為止。                 until such time as the assets are substantially ready for their
                          intended use or sale.


 於有關資產可供擬定用途或出售後仍未償還      Any specific borrowing that remain outstanding after the related
 的任何特定借款計入一般借貸組合以計算一      asset is ready for its intended use or sale is included in the
 般借款的資本化率。尚未用於合資格資產的      general borrowing pool for calculation of capitalisation rate on
 特定借貸作暫時投資賺取的投資收入，於合      general borrowings. Investment income earned on the temporary
 資格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。           investment of specific borrowings pending their expenditure on
                          qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing costs eligible for
                          capitalisation.


 所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間在損益中確      All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the
 認。                       period in which they are incurred.


 政府補貼                     Government grants
 在合理地保證本集團會遵守政府補貼的附帶      Government grants are not recognised until there is reasonable
 條件，以及將會得到補助前，不會確認政府      assurance that the Group will comply with the conditions
 補貼。                      attaching to them and that the grants will be received.


 與收入相關的政府補貼乃按系統基準，於本      Government grants related to income are recognised in profit or
 集團將有關補助擬補償的有關成本確認為開      loss on a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group
 支的期間內，於損益中確認。具體而言，以      recognises as expenses the related costs for which the grants are
 要求本集團購買、建造或收購非流動資產為      intended to compensate. Specifically, government grants of which
 主要條件的政府補貼乃於綜合財務狀況表中      the primary condition is that the Group should purchase, construct

 期內按系統及合理基準轉撥至損益中。        income in the consolidated statement of financial position and
                          transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and rational basis over
                          the useful lives of the related assets.

3. 主要會計政策（續）           3. Significant accounting policies (Continued)
 於聯營公司的權益                 Interest in an associate
 聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力的實       An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant
 體。重大影響力指有權參與被投資方的財務      influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the
 及經營政策的決策，但對該等政策並無控制      financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is not
 或共同控制權。                  control or joint control over those policies.


 聯營公司之業績、資產及負債使用權益會計      The results and assets and liabilities of associates are incorporated
 法計入此等綜合財務報表。以權益會計法處      in these consolidated financial statements using the equity
 理的聯營公司財務報表乃按本集團就於類似      method of accounting. The financial statements of associates
 情況下的交易及事件所採用者相同的會計政      used for equity accounting purposes are prepared using uniform
 策編製。根據權益法，於聯營公司之投資初      accounting policies as those of the Group for like transactions
 步按成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，並於其後      and events in similar circumstances. Under the equity method,
 就確認本集團應佔該聯營公司之損益及其他      an investment in an associate is initially recognised in the
 全面收益而作出調整。當本集團分佔聯營公      consolidated statement of financial position at cost and adjusted
 司之虧損超出本集團於該聯營公司之權益時      thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the profit or loss
 （包括實質上成為本集團於該聯營公司投資      and other comprehensive income of the associate. When the
 淨額一部分之任何長期權益），本集團終止      Group’s share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s
 確認其所佔進一步虧損。僅於本集團已產生      interest in that associate (which includes any long-term interests
 法律或推定責任，或已代表該聯營公司支付      that, in substance, form part of the Group’s net investment in the
 款項之情況下，方會進一步確認虧損。        associate), the Group discontinues recognizing its share of further
                          losses. Additional losses are recognised only to the extent that
                          the Group has incurred legal or constructive obligations or made
                          payments on behalf of the associate.


 於被投資方成為一間聯營公司當日，對聯營      An investment in an associate is accounted for using the equity
 公司之投資採用權益法入賬。於收購一間聯      method from the date on which the investee becomes an
 營公司之投資時，投資成本超過本集團分佔      associate. On acquisition of the investment in an associate, any
 該被投資方可識別資產及負債公平淨值之任      excess of the cost of the investment over the Group’s share of
 何部分乃確認為商譽，並計入投資之賬面       the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities of the
 值。本集團所佔可識別資產及負債於重新評      investee is recognised as goodwill, which is included within the
 估後之公平淨值與投資成本之任何差額，會      carrying amount of the investment. Any excess of the Group’s
 於收購投資期間即時於損益確認。          share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities
                          over the cost of the investment, after reassessment, is recognised
                          immediately in profit or loss in the period in which the investment
                          is acquired.

 金融工具                      Financial instruments

 時，確認金融資產及金融負債。所有以常規       group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the

 實際利息法是一種計算金融資產或金融負債       The effective interest method is a method of calculating the
 的攤銷成本及於有關期間攤分利息收入及利       amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of
 息開支的方法。實際利率是於初步確認時準       allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant
 確地透過金融資產或金融負債預計年期或        period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts
 （如適用）於較短期間內將估計日後現金收款      estimated future cash receipts and payments (including all fees
 及付款（包括屬整體實際利率一部分的所有       and points paid or received that form an integral part of the
 已付或已收費用及利息、交易成本及其他溢       effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or
 價或折讓）貼現為賬面淨值的利率。          discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or
                           financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the
                           net carrying amount on initial recognition.

      資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財務困難及無             The Group writes off a financial asset when there is

 (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認            (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL
       預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違約              The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability
       損失率（即違約損失程度）及違約風險的             of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the
       函數。違約概率及違約損失率乃基於根              loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The
       據前瞻性資料調整的歷史數據評估。預              assessment of the probability of default and loss given
       期信貸虧損的預估乃無偏概率加權平均              default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-
       金額，以發生違約的風險為權重確定。              looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased
                                      and probability-weighted amount that is determined with
                                      the respective risks of default occurring as the weights.


       一般而言，預期信貸虧損按本集團根據              Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual
       合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本集團              cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with
       預計收取的現金流量的差額估計，並按              the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to
       初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。                receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined
                                      at initial recognition.

       商譽減值評估                                    Impairment assessment of goodwill
       確定商譽是否減值須估計獲分配有關商譽之                       Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an estimation
       現金產生單位（或一組現金產生單位）可收回                      of the recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit (or group
       金額，即使用價值或公平值減出售成本兩者                       of cash-generating units) to which goodwill has been allocated,
       間的較高者。在計算使用價值時，本集團須                       which is the higher of the value in use or fair value less costs

 6.2 經營分部                    6.2 Operating Segments
   本集團業務乃按產品及物業開發的類型            The Group’s operations are organised based on the different
   劃分。向本公司董事會（即總營運決策            types of products and property development. Information
   人）
    （「總營運決策人」）報告以作資源分           reported to the board of directors of the Company, being the
   配及分部表現評估的資料亦按產品及物            chief operating decision maker (“CODM”), for the purposes of
   業開發的類型呈列。此乃本集團之構造            resource allocation and assessment of segment performance
   基礎。                          focuses on types of products and property development.
                                This is the basis upon which the Group is organised.

   本集團之經營及呈報分部如下：               The Group’s operating and reportable segments are as
                                follows:

   －   高分子材料；                   –      Polymers;

   －   製冷劑；                     –      Refrigerants;

   －   有機硅；                     –      Organic silicon;

   －   二氯甲烷、PVC及燒鹼；             –      Dichloromethane, PVC and liquid alkali;

   －   物業開發－在中國山東省及湖南           –      Property development – development of residential
       省發展住宅物業；及                       properties in Shandong and Hunan Province, the PRC;
                                       and

   －   其他業務－生產及銷售製冷劑分           –      Other operations – manufacturing and sales of side-
       部、高分子材料分部、有機硅分                  products of refrigerants segment, polymers segment,
       部及二氯甲烷、PVC及燒鹼分部的                organic silicon segment and dichloromethane, PVC
       副產品，及租金收入。                      and liquid alkali segment and rental income.

 就兩個年度而言，本集團就營運租賃多個                               For both years, the Group leases various offices for its operations.
 辦公室。租賃合約簽訂的固定期限為兩年                               Lease contracts are entered into for fixed term of 2 years (2020:
 （2020年：兩年）。租賃條款乃根據個別情況                           2 years). Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and
 協商，並含不同的條款及條件。在確定租賃                              contain different terms and conditions. In determining the lease
 期及評估不可撤銷期間的長度時，本集團應                              term and assessing the length of the non-cancellable period, the
 用合約的定義並釐定合約的可執行期限。                               Group applies the definition of a contract and determines the
                                                  period for which the contract is enforceable.


  本集團為相關租賃土地的註冊擁有人。為收                             The Group is the registered owner of the underlying leasehold
  購該等物業權益，一筆款項已預先支付。僅                             lands. Lump sum payments were made upfront to acquire these
  在支付的款項能夠可靠分配的情況下，該等                             property interests. The leasehold land components of these

27. 貿易及其他應收款項（續）                                 27. Trade and other receivables (Continued)
       在承接任何新客戶前，本集團會調查新客戶                            Before accepting any new customer, the Group conducts research
       的信譽及評估潛在客戶的信貸質素和確定客                            on the creditworthiness of the new customer and assesses the
       戶的信用額度。客戶的信用額度和信用評級                            potential customer’s credit quality and defines credit limits by
       每年複核兩次。99%（2020年：99%）未逾期                       customer. Limits and scoring attributed to customers are reviewed
       亦未減值之貿易應收款項及應收票據擁有信                            twice a year. 99% (2020: 99%) of the trade and bills receivables
       譽調查的良好信用評級。本集團視乎客戶合                            that are neither past due nor impaired have the high-ranking
       約金額、信用及聲譽提供多種還款期。                              record attributable under the research on the creditworthiness.
                                                      The Group offers various settlement terms which vary depending
                                                      on the size of contract, credibility and reputation of the customers.

37. 退休福利計劃                                 37. Retirement benefits plans
       本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府營                    The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of state-
       運的國家退休福利計劃的成員。本集團須向                    managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the respective
       計劃供款指定百分比（按薪酬成本計算），作                   local governments in the PRC. The Group is required to contribute
       為有關福利的資金。本集團的唯一責任是對                    a specified percentage of payroll costs to the schemes to fund the
       該等計劃作出指定供款。                            benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect to these
                                              schemes is to make the specified contributions.

41. 視為部分出售一間附屬公司                              41. Deemed partial disposal of a subsidiary
       於2020年3月12日，本集團完成山東東岳有                      On 12 March 2020, the Group has completed the spin-off
       機硅材料股份有限公司（「東岳有機硅」）的                        of Shangdong Dongyue Organosilicon Co., Ltd (“Dongyue
       股票在深圳證券交易所的創業板分拆上市                          Organosilicon”), an indirect non-wholly owned subsidiary of the
      （「分拆」），該公司是本公司的間接非全資                         Company engaged in manufacture and sale of organosilicon
       附屬公司，從事有機硅材料的生產和銷售。                         material, through a separate listing of its shares on the ChiNext
       分 拆 涉 及 以 發 行 價 每 股 人 民 幣6.90元 發 行          of Shenzhen Stock Exchange (“Spin-Off”). The Spin-Off involved
       300,000,000股每股面值人民幣1元的新A股，                  offering of 300,000,000 new A-share of RMB1 each at an issue
       總計籌得約人民幣1,985,849,000元的淨現金                  price of RMB6.90 per share, which raised a total net cash proceeds
       收益。                                         of approximately RMB1,985,849,000.


       緊接分拆完成後，本集團在東岳有機硅的股                         Immediately following the completion of the Spin-Off, the Group’s
       權隨即從77%攤薄至57.75%，因此分拆被視                     equity interest in Dongyue Organosilicon was diluted from 77%
       為部分出售。由於視為部分出售東岳有機硅                         to 57.75% and thus the Spin-Off is considered as a deemed
       不會導致失去控制權，因此該交易作股權交                         partial disposal. Since the deemed partial disposal of Dongyue
       易入賬，而分拆所得淨額與當時東岳有機                          Organosilicon did not result in any loss of control, the transaction
       硅42.25%賬面值的差額被確認為本集團的權                      was accounted for as an equity transaction and the difference
       益。                                          between the net proceeds from the Spin-Off and the then 42.25%
                                                   carrying value of Dongyue Organosilicon is recognised in the
                                                   equity of the Group.


       分拆對財務影響總結如下：                                A summary of the financial impacts of the Spin-Off are as follows:
